[[key_manager-address-map]]
key_manager address map
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

* Absolute Address: 0x0
* Base Offset: 0x0
* Size: 0x1B044

Complete memory map for the Key Manager subsystem, including ROM, SRAM,
control/status registers, mailbox, and external crypto engine ports.
This address map is from the perspective of the KM CPU (PicoRV32).

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|========================================================
|Offset |Identifier |Name
|0x0D000 |kpv |Key and Policy Vault (KM Port)
|0x0E000 |kmcsr |Key Manager Control and Status Registers
|0x0F000 |drbg_sampler |DRBG Sampler
|0x10000 |mailbox_km |Key Manager Mailbox KM Side
|0x18000 |otbn_wrapper_key |OTBN Wrapper Key Registers
|0x19000 |aes_wrapper_key |AES Wrapper Key Registers
|0x1A000 |kmac_wrapper_key |KMAC Wrapper Key Registers
|0x1B000 |hmac_wrapper_key |HMAC Wrapper Key Registers
|========================================================

[[word-register]]
WORD register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xD000
* Base Offset: 0x0
* Size: 0x4
* Array Dimensions: [16]
* Array Stride: 0x4
* Total Size: 0x40

One 32-bit word of a key entry (512-bit key = 16 words). No reset;
powers up random.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |data |rw |— |DATA
|=====================================

[[data-field]]
data field
++++++++++

32-bit key data word. No reset for security; power-up value undefined.
Actual storage is in km_kpv_regfile; CSR field is a protocol
placeholder.

[[ctrl-register]]
CTRL register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xD800
* Base Offset: 0x800
* Size: 0x4
* Array Dimensions: [32]
* Array Stride: 0x4
* Total Size: 0x80

Per-slot control: lock bits (W1S), Extend, Dest_valid, Last_dword

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=========================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |lock_write |rw, woset |0x0 |LOCK_WRITE
|1 |lock_use |rw, woset |0x0 |LOCK_USE
|2 |unlock_sep |rw, woset |0x0 |UNLOCK_SEP
|3 |rsvd_3 |r |0x0 |RSVD_3
|6:4 |extend |rw |0x0 |EXTEND
|8:7 |rsvd_8_7 |r |0x0 |RSVD_8_7
|16:9 |dest_valid |rw |0x0 |DEST_VALID
|20:17 |last_dword |rw |0x0 |LAST_DWORD
|31:21 |rsvd_31_21 |r |0x0 |RSVD_31_21
|=========================================

[[lock_write-field]]
lock_write field
++++++++++++++++

Prevents KM write to this key entry data and control until reset.
Read-any, write-1-only.

[[lock_use-field]]
lock_use field
++++++++++++++

Prevents KM read of this key entry data until reset. Read-any,
write-1-only.

[[unlock_sep-field]]
unlock_sep field
++++++++++++++++

Allows SEP (KPVLP) to write this slot when lock_write==0. Read-any,
write-1-only.

[[rsvd_3-field]]
rsvd_3 field
++++++++++++

Reserved

[[extend-field]]
extend field
++++++++++++

Zero-indexed number of additional slots for wide keys. Firmware
enforced.

[[rsvd_8_7-field]]
rsvd_8_7 field
++++++++++++++

Reserved

[[dest_valid-field]]
dest_valid field
++++++++++++++++

Which crypto block may consume this key. Firmware enforced.

[[last_dword-field]]
last_dword field
++++++++++++++++

Last valid key word index [0,15]. Hardware returns 0 for reads beyond
this.

[[rsvd_31_21-field]]
rsvd_31_21 field
++++++++++++++++

Reserved

[[kpv_scrambler_key-register]]
KPV_SCRAMBLER_KEY register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xD880
* Base Offset: 0x880
* Size: 0x4

32-bit key for KPV key entry scrambling. No reset; powers up random.
Locked when KPV_SCRAMBLER_CTRL.LOCK=1.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |key |rw |— |KEY
|=====================================

[[key-field]]
key field
+++++++++

Scrambler key. No reset for security. HW clears to 0 on wipe via hwclr
(FR-0000-143).

[[kpv_scrambler_ctrl-register]]
KPV_SCRAMBLER_CTRL register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xD884
* Base Offset: 0x884
* Size: 0x4

KPV scrambler enable and lock. Reset to 0 on KM reset. LOCK is
write-one-only.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |enable |rw |0x0 |ENABLE
|1 |lock |rw, woset |0x0 |LOCK
|31:2 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[enable-field]]
enable field
++++++++++++

1 = scramble key entry data on write, descramble on read; 0 =
passthrough.

[[lock-field]]
lock field
++++++++++

Write-one-only. When 1, key and ENABLE cannot be modified until KM
reset.

[[rsvd-field]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[kmcsr-address-map]]
kmcsr address map
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

* Absolute Address: 0xE000
* Base Offset: 0xE000
* Size: 0x200

Control and status registers for the Key Manager MVP subsystem

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|===========================================================
|Offset |Identifier |Name
|0x000 |VERSION |VERSION
|0x004 |CTRL |CTRL
|0x008 |SOFT_RST_CODE |SOFT_RST_CODE
|0x00C |IRQ_STATUS |IRQ_STATUS
|0x010 |IRQ_ENABLE |IRQ_ENABLE
|0x014 |SCRAMBLER_KEY |SCRAMBLER_KEY
|0x018 |SCRAMBLER_CTRL |SCRAMBLER_CTRL
|0x01C |SRAM_LOCK |SRAM_LOCK
|0x020 |IRQ_SET |IRQ_SET
|0x024 |SRAM_WRITE_LOCK_VIOLATION |SRAM_WRITE_LOCK_VIOLATION
|0x028 |RECOVERABLE_ERR |RECOVERABLE_ERR
|0x02C |BOOT_STATUS |BOOT_STATUS
|0x030 |OTP_LIFE_CYCLE |OTP_LIFE_CYCLE
|0x034 |OTP_DEMOTION_STATE |OTP_DEMOTION_STATE
|0x038 |OTP_CHIPLET_UID_0 |OTP_CHIPLET_UID_BYTE
|0x03C |OTP_CHIPLET_UID_1 |OTP_CHIPLET_UID_BYTE

[[version-register]]
VERSION register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE000
* Base Offset: 0x0
* Size: 0x4

Hardware version register (semantic versioning: major.minor.patch)

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |patch |r |0x0 |PATCH
|15:8 |minor |r |0x0 |MINOR
|23:16 |major |r |0x1 |MAJOR
|31:24 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[patch-field]]
patch field
+++++++++++

Patch version number

[[minor-field]]
minor field
+++++++++++

Minor version number

[[major-field]]
major field
+++++++++++

Major version number

[[rsvd-field-1]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved; must read as zero

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD

rsvd field

[[soft_rst_code-register]]
SOFT_RST_CODE register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE008
* Base Offset: 0x8
* Size: 0x4

Software reset code register. Write 0x53525354 (`SRST') to trigger soft
reset. Any other value has no effect.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |code |rw |0x0 |CODE
|=====================================

[[code-field]]
code field
++++++++++

32-bit reset code. Write 0x53525354 to trigger soft reset.

[[irq_status-register]]
IRQ_STATUS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE00C
* Base Offset: 0xC
* Size: 0x4

Interrupt status register. Sticky bits cleared by writing 1.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|===========================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |rom_parity_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |ROM_PARITY_ERR
|1 |sram_parity_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |SRAM_PARITY_ERR
|2 |rom_write_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |ROM_WRITE_ERR
|3 |sram_write_lock_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |SRAM_WRITE_LOCK_ERR
|4 |axi_slverr |rw, woclr |0x0 |AXI_SLVERR
|5 |axi_decerr |rw, woclr |0x0 |AXI_DECERR
|6 |drbg_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |DRBG_ERR
|7 |wipe_state |rw, woclr |0x0 |WIPE_STATE
|31:8 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|===========================================================

[[rom_parity_err-field]]
rom_parity_err field
++++++++++++++++++++

ROM parity error detected. Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[sram_parity_err-field]]
sram_parity_err field
+++++++++++++++++++++

SRAM parity error detected. Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[rom_write_err-field]]
rom_write_err field
+++++++++++++++++++

ROM write attempt detected (ROM is read-only). Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[sram_write_lock_err-field]]
sram_write_lock_err field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++

SRAM write attempt to a write-locked region detected. Sticky, write 1 to
clear.

[[axi_slverr-field]]
axi_slverr field
++++++++++++++++

AXI SLVERR (slave error) response detected on CPU bus transaction.
Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[axi_decerr-field]]
axi_decerr field
++++++++++++++++

AXI DECERR (decode error) response detected on CPU bus transaction.
Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[drbg_err-field]]
drbg_err field
++++++++++++++

DRBG Sampler error (timeout or AXI-Stream error). Sticky, write 1 to
clear.

[[wipe_state-field]]
wipe_state field
++++++++++++++++

Wipe state event (rising edge of wipe_state input). Sticky, write 1 to
clear.

[[rsvd-field-3]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

Interrupt enable/mask register

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |rom_parity_en |rw |0x0 |ROM_PARITY_EN
|1 |sram_parity_en |rw |0x0 |SRAM_PARITY_EN
|2 |rom_write_en |rw |0x0 |ROM_WRITE_EN

|31:8 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|==================================================

[[rom_parity_en-field]]
rom_parity_en field
+++++++++++++++++++

Enable ROM parity error interrupt

[[sram_parity_en-field]]
sram_parity_en field
++++++++++++++++++++

Enable SRAM parity error interrupt

[[rom_write_en-field]]
rom_write_en field
++++++++++++++++++

Enable ROM write error interrupt

[[sram_write_lock_en-field]]
sram_write_lock_en field
++++++++++++++++++++++++

Enable SRAM write-lock violation interrupt

[[axi_slverr_en-field]]
axi_slverr_en field
+++++++++++++++++++

Enable AXI SLVERR error interrupt

[[axi_decerr_en-field]]
axi_decerr_en field
+++++++++++++++++++

Enable AXI DECERR error interrupt

[[drbg_err_en-field]]
drbg_err_en field
+++++++++++++++++

Enable DRBG Sampler error interrupt

[[wipe_state_en-field]]
wipe_state_en field
+++++++++++++++++++

[[rsvd-field-4]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[scrambler_key-register]]
SCRAMBLER_KEY register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE014
* Base Offset: 0x14
* Size: 0x4

32-bit scrambler key for SRAM address/data scrambling. When locked:
writes ignored, reads return 0. No reset; powers up random.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |key |rw |— |KEY
|=====================================

[[key-field-1]]
key field
+++++++++

Scrambler key value. Not readable when SCRAMBLER_CTRL.LOCK=1 (returns
0). No reset for security; power-up value undefined.

[[scrambler_ctrl-register]]
SCRAMBLER_CTRL register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE018
* Base Offset: 0x18
* Size: 0x4

Scrambler control register

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |enable |rw |0x0 |ENABLE
|1 |lock |rw |0x0 |LOCK
|31:2 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[enable-field-1]]
enable field
++++++++++++

Enable SRAM scrambling. When 0, data passes through unmodified. Locked
when LOCK=1 (security requirement).

[[lock-field-1]]
lock field
++++++++++

Lock scrambler key and enable bit. Once set, key and enable cannot be
modified until reset. Write-once (0->1 only). Security requirement:
prevents disabling scrambling after key provisioning.

[[rsvd-field-5]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[sram_lock-register]]
SRAM_LOCK register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE01C
* Base Offset: 0x1C
* Size: 0x4

SRAM write-lock bits. Bit=1 locks region i (512 bytes each, 32 regions).
Write-1-only: writing 1 sets the bit, writing 0 has no effect. Cleared
only by full reset.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|==========================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |lock_bits |rw, woset |0x0 |LOCK_BITS
|==========================================

[[lock_bits-field]]
lock_bits field
+++++++++++++++

One bit per 512-byte SRAM region. Region 0 = 0x4000-0x41FF, region 31 =
0x7E00-0x7FFF. Write 1 to lock; 0 has no effect.

[[irq_set-register]]
IRQ_SET register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE020
* Base Offset: 0x20
* Size: 0x4

Interrupt set register. Write 1 to trigger corresponding interrupt (for
ISR testing). Write-only, reads return 0.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|===========================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |rom_parity_err_set |w |0x0 |ROM_PARITY_ERR_SET
|1 |sram_parity_err_set |w |0x0 |SRAM_PARITY_ERR_SET
|2 |rom_write_err_set |w |0x0 |ROM_WRITE_ERR_SET
|3 |sram_write_lock_err_set |w |0x0 |SRAM_WRITE_LOCK_ERR_SET
|4 |axi_slverr_set |w |0x0 |AXI_SLVERR_SET
|5 |axi_decerr_set |w |0x0 |AXI_DECERR_SET
|6 |drbg_err_set |w |0x0 |DRBG_ERR_SET
|7 |wipe_state_set |w |0x0 |WIPE_STATE_SET
|31:8 |rsvd |w |0x0 |RSVD
|===========================================================

[[rom_parity_err_set-field]]
rom_parity_err_set field
++++++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.ROM_PARITY_ERR (triggers ROM parity interrupt
for testing)

[[sram_parity_err_set-field]]
sram_parity_err_set field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.SRAM_PARITY_ERR (triggers SRAM parity
interrupt for testing)

[[rom_write_err_set-field]]
rom_write_err_set field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.ROM_WRITE_ERR (triggers ROM write interrupt
for testing)

[[sram_write_lock_err_set-field]]
sram_write_lock_err_set field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.SRAM_WRITE_LOCK_ERR (triggers SRAM write-lock
interrupt for testing)

[[axi_slverr_set-field]]
axi_slverr_set field
++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.AXI_SLVERR (triggers AXI SLVERR interrupt for
testing)

[[axi_decerr_set-field]]
axi_decerr_set field
++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.AXI_DECERR (triggers AXI DECERR interrupt for
testing)

[[drbg_err_set-field]]
drbg_err_set field
++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.DRBG_ERR (triggers DRBG Sampler interrupt for
testing)

[[wipe_state_set-field]]
wipe_state_set field
++++++++++++++++++++

Write 1 to set IRQ_STATUS.WIPE_STATE (triggers wipe state interrupt for
testing)

[[rsvd-field-6]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved (write-only; reads trigger SLVERR via err_if_bad_rw)

[[sram_write_lock_violation-register]]
SRAM_WRITE_LOCK_VIOLATION register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE024
* Base Offset: 0x24
* Size: 0x4

Which SRAM region(s) had write attempts while locked. Bit=region i.
Sticky, write 1 to clear each bit.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|====================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |violation_bits |rw, woclr |0x0 |VIOLATION_BITS
|====================================================

[[violation_bits-field]]
violation_bits field
++++++++++++++++++++

Bit=1 if region i had attempted write while locked. Sticky, write 1 to
clear each bit.

[[recoverable_err-register]]
RECOVERABLE_ERR register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE028
* Base Offset: 0x28
* Size: 0x4

Recoverable error status. Firmware writes 1 to set (after recovering in
ISR), writes 0 to clear (e.g. when SEP requests clear). Drives
recoverable error event output.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|============================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |recoverable_err |rw |0x0 |RECOVERABLE_ERR
|31:1 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|============================================

[[recoverable_err-field]]
recoverable_err field
+++++++++++++++++++++

1 = recoverable fault occurred and was handled by ISR; 0 = clear. Write
1 to set, write 0 to clear.

[[rsvd-field-7]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[boot_status-register]]
BOOT_STATUS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE02C
* Base Offset: 0x2C
* Size: 0x4

Boot status. Firmware writes 1 to COLD_BOOT_DONE after cold-boot init
completes; bit remains set until next cold reset.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |cold_boot_done |rw, woset |0x0 |COLD_BOOT_DONE
|31:1 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=================================================

[[cold_boot_done-field]]
cold_boot_done field
++++++++++++++++++++

Write-1-only: set by ROM firmware at end of cold boot. Reads 0 after
cold reset; reads 1 after firmware sets it. Sticky until next cold reset
(preserved across warm resets).

[[rsvd-field-8]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[irq_entry_addr-register]]
IRQ_ENTRY_ADDR register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE0B8
* Base Offset: 0xB8
* Size: 0x4

Effective IRQ entry address for subsequent interrupts. Writable while
unlocked; any 32-bit value accepted.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |addr |rw |0x10 |ADDR
|=====================================

[[addr-field]]
addr field
++++++++++

IRQ handler entry PC. Reset matches ROM vector at 0x10. When locked, SW
writes do not update storage.

[[irq_entry_lock-register]]
IRQ_ENTRY_LOCK register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE0BC
* Base Offset: 0xBC
* Size: 0x4

Write-one-only lock for IRQ_ENTRY_ADDR. Separate readable register;
cleared only on KM reset.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |lock |rw, woset |0x0 |LOCK
|31:1 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[lock-field-2]]
lock field
++++++++++

Write 1 to set; write 0 has no effect; sticky until reset.

[[rsvd-field-43]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved; reads as zero.

[[vuart_tx-register]]
VUART_TX register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE100
* Base Offset: 0x100
* Size: 0x4

Virtual UART transmit register. Write a byte to send to testbench.
DATA_VALID is set on write and self-clears.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |tx_byte |rw |0x0 |TX_BYTE
|30:8 |rsvd0 |r |0x0 |RSVD0
|31 |data_valid |rw |0x0 |DATA_VALID
|=====================================

[[tx_byte-field]]
tx_byte field
+++++++++++++

Byte to transmit (write to send character to testbench)

[[rsvd0-field]]
rsvd0 field
+++++++++++

Reserved

[[data_valid-field]]
data_valid field
++++++++++++++++

Data valid strobe. Set by firmware on write, cleared by hardware after
one cycle.

[[vuart_rx-register]]
VUART_RX register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE104
* Base Offset: 0x104
* Size: 0x4

Virtual UART receive register. Testbench writes bytes here for firmware
to read.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|7:0 |rx_byte |r |0x0 |RX_BYTE
|30:8 |rsvd0 |r |0x0 |RSVD0
|31 |data_valid |r |0x0 |DATA_VALID
|=====================================

[[rx_byte-field]]
rx_byte field
+++++++++++++

Received byte from testbench

[[rsvd0-field-1]]
rsvd0 field
+++++++++++

Reserved

[[data_valid-field-1]]
data_valid field
++++++++++++++++

RX data valid. Set by testbench, cleared by firmware read.

[[vuart_status-register]]
VUART_STATUS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xE108
* Base Offset: 0x108
* Size: 0x4

Virtual UART status register for flow control

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |tx_ready |r |0x1 |TX_READY
|1 |rx_valid |r |0x0 |RX_VALID
|2 |print_enable |r |0x0 |PRINT_ENABLE
|31:3 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[tx_ready-field]]
tx_ready field
++++++++++++++

TX ready to accept data (always 1 in simulation)

[[rx_valid-field]]
rx_valid field
++++++++++++++

RX has valid data available

[[print_enable-field]]
print_enable field
++++++++++++++++++

Enable VUART printing. Set by testbench to enable printf output.
Disabled by default to save simulation time.

[[rsvd-field-44]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |signature |rw |0x0 |SIGNATURE

Debug register with known constant value for verification

|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |magic |r |0xCAFEBEEF |MAGIC
|=====================================

magic field
+++++++++++

Magic constant: 0xCAFEBEEF

[[drbg_sampler-address-map]]
drbg_sampler address map
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

* Absolute Address: 0xF000
* Base Offset: 0xF000
* Size: 0x10

DRBG random data interface and configuration/status registers

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|=================================
|Offset |Identifier |Name
|0x0 |DATA |DATA
|0x4 |CFG |CFG
|0x8 |STATUS |STATUS
|0xC |PREFETCH_DATA |PREFETCH_DATA
|=================================

[[data-register]]
DATA register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xF000
* Base Offset: 0x0
* Size: 0x4

One word of random data. Read triggers request or returns prefetch.
Writes return SLVERR. No reset.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |data |r |— |DATA
|=====================================

[[data-field-512]]
data field
++++++++++

32-bit random data from DRBG. No reset for security; power-up value
undefined.

[[cfg-register]]
CFG register
^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xF004
* Base Offset: 0x4
* Size: 0x4

Configuration: PREFETCH enable, TIMEOUT cycles for active CPU read.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |prefetch |rw |0x0 |PREFETCH
|15:1 |rsvd |rw |0x0 |RSVD
|31:16 |timeout |rw |0x100 |TIMEOUT
|=====================================

[[prefetch-field]]
prefetch field
++++++++++++++

Enable prefetch. When 0, prefetch data register is cleared.

[[rsvd-field-45]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[timeout-field]]
timeout field
+++++++++++++

Cycles to wait for DRBG on active CPU read; 0 = disable. Default 256.

[[status-register]]
STATUS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xF008
* Base Offset: 0x8
* Size: 0x4

Status: DRBG_READY, PREFETCHED, TIMEOUT_ERR (W1C), STREAM_ERR (W1C),
COUNT_BAD, COUNT_GOOD.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|===========================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |drbg_ready |r |0x0 |DRBG_READY
|1 |prefetched |r |0x0 |PREFETCHED
|2 |timeout_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |TIMEOUT_ERR
|3 |stream_err |rw, woclr |0x0 |STREAM_ERR
|7:4 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|15:8 |count_bad |r |0x0 |COUNT_BAD
|31:16 |count_good |r |0x0 |COUNT_GOOD
|===========================================

[[drbg_ready-field]]
drbg_ready field
++++++++++++++++

TVALID asserted from DRBG

[[prefetched-field]]
prefetched field
++++++++++++++++

Prefetched data available

[[timeout_err-field]]
timeout_err field
+++++++++++++++++

Set when a DRBG read from the KM CPU times out. Write 1 to clear.

[[stream_err-field]]
stream_err field
++++++++++++++++

Set when there is an error on the DRBG AXI-Stream interface (e.g. TVALID
deasserted before TREADY). Write 1 to clear.

[[rsvd-field-46]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[count_bad-field]]
count_bad field
+++++++++++++++

Failed transactions (timeout or error). Saturates at 0xFF.

[[count_good-field]]
count_good field
++++++++++++++++

Successful words transferred to CPU. Saturates at 0xFFFF.

[[prefetch_data-register]]
PREFETCH_DATA register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0xF00C
* Base Offset: 0xC
* Size: 0x4

Prefetched word (read-only, for debugging). Cleared when PREFETCH=0. No
reset; power-up undefined.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |data |r |— |DATA
|=====================================

[[data-field-513]]
data field
++++++++++

Prefetched 32-bit random data. No reset for security; power-up value
undefined.

[[mailbox_km-address-map]]
mailbox_km address map
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

* Absolute Address: 0x10000
* Base Offset: 0x10000
* Size: 0x1C

Register interface for KM CPU to access mailbox (write to outbound FIFO,
read from inbound FIFO)

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|=========================================
|Offset |Identifier |Name
|0x00 |KM_WRITE_DATA |WRITE_DATA
|0x04 |KM_WRITE_SEPARATOR |WRITE_SEPARATOR
|0x08 |KM_READ_DATA |READ_DATA
|0x0C |KM_STATUS |STATUS
|0x10 |KM_IRQ_STATUS |IRQ_STATUS
|0x14 |KM_IRQ_ENABLE |IRQ_ENABLE
|0x18 |KM_CTRL |CTRL
|=========================================

[[km_write_data-register]]
KM_WRITE_DATA register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10000
* Base Offset: 0x0
* Size: 0x4

Write data to outbound FIFO (KM→SEP messages)

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |data |w |0x0 |DATA
|=====================================

[[data-field-514]]
data field
++++++++++

32-bit data word to write to outbound FIFO

[[km_write_separator-register]]
KM_WRITE_SEPARATOR register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10004
* Base Offset: 0x4
* Size: 0x4

Write 1 to set message separator on next outbound (KM→SEP) write.
Cleared by hardware when that write completes.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |set |rw |0x0 |SET
|31:1 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[set-field]]
set field
+++++++++

Write 1 to set message separator on next outbound write. Cleared by
hardware when that write completes.

[[rsvd-field-47]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[km_read_data-register]]
KM_READ_DATA register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10008
* Base Offset: 0x8
* Size: 0x4

Read data from inbound FIFO (SEP→KM messages)

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|31:0 |data |r |0x0 |DATA
|=====================================

[[data-field-515]]
data field
++++++++++

32-bit data word read from inbound FIFO

[[km_status-register]]
KM_STATUS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1000C
* Base Offset: 0xC
* Size: 0x4

FIFO status information

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|==========================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |inbound_empty |r |0x1 |INBOUND_EMPTY
|1 |inbound_full |r |0x0 |INBOUND_FULL
|2 |outbound_empty |r |0x1 |OUTBOUND_EMPTY
|3 |outbound_full |r |0x0 |OUTBOUND_FULL
|11:4 |inbound_depth |r |0x0 |INBOUND_DEPTH
|19:12 |outbound_depth |r |0x0 |OUTBOUND_DEPTH
|20 |inbound_overflow |rw, woclr |0x0 |INBOUND_OVERFLOW
|21 |outbound_overflow |rw, woclr |0x0 |OUTBOUND_OVERFLOW
|22 |inbound_underflow |rw, woclr |0x0 |INBOUND_UNDERFLOW
|23 |outbound_underflow |rw, woclr |0x0 |OUTBOUND_UNDERFLOW
|24 |inbound_separator |r |0x0 |INBOUND_SEPARATOR
|25 |outbound_separator |r |0x0 |OUTBOUND_SEPARATOR
|31:26 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|==========================================================

[[inbound_empty-field]]
inbound_empty field
+++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO is empty (no data available for KM to read)

[[inbound_full-field]]
inbound_full field
++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO is full

[[outbound_empty-field]]
outbound_empty field
++++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO is empty (no data available for SEP to read)

[[outbound_full-field]]
outbound_full field
+++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO is full (cannot write more data)

[[inbound_depth-field]]
inbound_depth field
+++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO fill level (number of words in FIFO)

[[outbound_depth-field]]
outbound_depth field
++++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO fill level (number of words in FIFO)

[[inbound_overflow-field]]
inbound_overflow field
++++++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO overflow detected (write attempted when full). Sticky,
write 1 to clear.

[[outbound_overflow-field]]
outbound_overflow field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO overflow detected (write attempted when full). Sticky,
write 1 to clear.

[[inbound_underflow-field]]
inbound_underflow field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO underflow detected (read attempted when empty). Sticky,
write 1 to clear.

[[outbound_underflow-field]]
outbound_underflow field
++++++++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO underflow detected (read attempted when empty). Sticky,
write 1 to clear.

[[inbound_separator-field]]
inbound_separator field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Message separator: 1 if the last word read from inbound FIFO had the
separator bit set (last word of message). Read-only, not sticky.

[[outbound_separator-field]]
outbound_separator field
++++++++++++++++++++++++

Message separator: 1 if the last word read from outbound FIFO had the
separator bit set (last word of message). Read-only, not sticky.

[[rsvd-field-48]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[km_irq_status-register]]
KM_IRQ_STATUS register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10010
* Base Offset: 0x10
* Size: 0x4

Interrupt status register

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=================================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |inbound_read_data_avail |r |0x0 |INBOUND_READ_DATA_AVAIL
|1 |outbound_write_space_avail |r |0x0 |OUTBOUND_WRITE_SPACE_AVAIL
|2 |outbound_overflow |rw, woclr |0x0 |OUTBOUND_OVERFLOW
|3 |inbound_underflow |rw, woclr |0x0 |INBOUND_UNDERFLOW
|4 |flushed_by_sep |rw, woclr |0x0 |FLUSHED_BY_SEP
|31:5 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=================================================================

[[inbound_read_data_avail-field]]
inbound_read_data_avail field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO has data available for KM to read (level-sensitive). 1 =
data available, 0 = empty.

[[outbound_write_space_avail-field]]
outbound_write_space_avail field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO has space available for KM to write (level-sensitive). 1 =
space available, 0 = full.

[[outbound_overflow-field-1]]
outbound_overflow field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Outbound FIFO overflow detected. Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[inbound_underflow-field-1]]
inbound_underflow field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Inbound FIFO underflow detected. Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[flushed_by_sep-field]]
flushed_by_sep field
++++++++++++++++++++

SEP performed a mailbox flush. Sticky, write 1 to clear.

[[rsvd-field-49]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[km_irq_enable-register]]
KM_IRQ_ENABLE register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10014
* Base Offset: 0x14
* Size: 0x4

Interrupt enable register for KM CPU

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=======================================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |inbound_read_data_avail_en |rw |0x0 |INBOUND_READ_DATA_AVAIL_EN

|1 |outbound_write_space_avail_en |rw |0x0
|OUTBOUND_WRITE_SPACE_AVAIL_EN

|2 |outbound_overflow_en |rw |0x0 |OUTBOUND_OVERFLOW_EN

|3 |inbound_underflow_en |rw |0x0 |INBOUND_UNDERFLOW_EN

|4 |flushed_by_sep_en |rw |0x0 |FLUSHED_BY_SEP_EN

|31:5 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=======================================================================

[[inbound_read_data_avail_en-field]]
inbound_read_data_avail_en field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Enable interrupt to KM when inbound FIFO has data available (SEP->KM
messages)

[[outbound_write_space_avail_en-field]]
outbound_write_space_avail_en field
+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Enable interrupt to KM when outbound FIFO has space available (KM->SEP
messages)

[[outbound_overflow_en-field]]
outbound_overflow_en field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Enable interrupt to KM when outbound FIFO overflow is detected

[[inbound_underflow_en-field]]
inbound_underflow_en field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Enable interrupt to KM when inbound FIFO underflow is detected

[[flushed_by_sep_en-field]]
flushed_by_sep_en field
+++++++++++++++++++++++

Enable interrupt to KM when SEP performs a mailbox flush

[[rsvd-field-50]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[km_ctrl-register]]
KM_CTRL register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x10018
* Base Offset: 0x18
* Size: 0x4

Control register for mailbox behavior configuration

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|==========================================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |outbound_overflow_resp |rw |0x0 |OUTBOUND_OVERFLOW_RESP
|1 |inbound_underflow_resp |rw |0x0 |INBOUND_UNDERFLOW_RESP
|2 |flush |rw |0x0 |FLUSH
|31:3 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|==========================================================

[[outbound_overflow_resp-field]]
outbound_overflow_resp field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Response type for outbound FIFO overflow: 0=SLVERR (default), 1=OKAY

[[inbound_underflow_resp-field]]
inbound_underflow_resp field
++++++++++++++++++++++++++++

Response type for inbound FIFO underflow: 0=SLVERR (default), 1=OKAY

[[flush-field]]
flush field
+++++++++++

Write 1 to flush all mailbox FIFOs (inbound and outbound). Cleared by
hardware when flush completes.

[[rsvd-field-51]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[key_valid-field]]
key_valid field
+++++++++++++++

When 1, asserts keymgr_key_i.valid to indicate key is loaded. Write 0 to
invalidate the current key.

[[key_ctrl-register-2]]
KEY_CTRL register
^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

* Absolute Address: 0x1A040
* Base Offset: 0x40
* Size: 0x4

Key control register. Writing KEY_VALID=1 asserts the sideload valid
signal to the KMAC core.

[cols=",,,,",options="header",]
|=====================================
|Bits |Identifier |Access |Reset |Name
|0 |key_valid |rw |0x0 |KEY_VALID
|31:1 |rsvd |r |0x0 |RSVD
|=====================================

[[key_valid-field-2]]
key_valid field
+++++++++++++++

When 1, asserts keymgr_key_i.valid to indicate key is loaded. Write 0 to
invalidate the current key.

[[rsvd-field-54]]
rsvd field
++++++++++

Reserved

[[hmac_wrapper_key-address-map]]
hmac_wrapper_key address map
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

* Absolute Address: 0x1B000
* Base Offset: 0x1B000
* Size: 0x44

Key storage registers for the HMAC sideload interface. Written by Key
Manager CPU, drives keymgr_key_i on the OT HMAC core.

[cols=",,",options="header",]
|=============================
|Offset |Identifier |Name
|0x00 |KEY_SHARE0[0] |KEY_WORD
|0x04 |KEY_SHARE0[1] |KEY_WORD
|0x08 |KEY_SHARE0[2] |KEY_WORD
|0x0C |KEY_SHARE0[3] |KEY_WORD
|0x10 |KEY_SHARE0[4] |KEY_WORD
|0x14 |KEY_SHARE0[5] |KEY_WORD
|0x18 |KEY_SHARE0[6] |KEY_WORD
|0x1C |KEY_SHARE0[7] |KEY_WORD
|0x20 |KEY_SHARE1[0] |KEY_WORD
|0x24 |KEY_SHARE1[1] |KEY_WORD
|0x28 |KEY_SHARE1[2] |KEY_WORD
|0x2C |KEY_SHARE1[3] |KEY_WORD
|0x30 |KEY_SHARE1[4] |KEY_WORD
|0x34 |KEY_SHARE1[5] |KEY_WORD
|0x38 |KEY_SHARE1[6] |KEY_WORD
|0x3C |KEY_SHARE1[7] |KEY_WORD
|0x40 |KEY_CTRL |KEY_CTRL
|=============================
